(Katto Katnc Shaphang Kane Ka Kot) 


Kane ka long ka novel Khasi kaba kham pher, kaba 
pynpyrkhat (food for thought) ia ka irnlang sahlang (society) 
jong ngi kumba ngi long mynta. Ka longkur longkha ka synjor 
shibun namar ba jur ki jingtrei kiba nabar (external influences). 
Ka thoh ia ka jingini bapher bapher ha la ki iing ki iing, ne ki 
briew ki briew shynrang bad kynthei. Ki nikom pyrkhat bad 
leh (variety of thoughts and action) kiba ialam bakla bad 
pynpra ia ka longiing. Ka don ruh ha ka ia ki ktien Sohra 
(phrases from Sohra) kiba ai jinghikai shibun ia ngi kiba pule 
(didactic). Ka don mh ka biria kaba siat jingmut. 


Haba shim kyllum ia ki pasoh kiba don ha kanc ka kot 
lah ban ong kumne Ki kyrteng jong ki briew shynrang ne 
kynthei ki long kiba da don jingmut. U nongthoh u la hei shitom 
ban pynwan ia kine. Ka dor jong ka kot ka jylliew bad ktah ia 
ka pyrkhat pyrdain jong ki por mynta ba ngi hiar shibun, ba ngi 
duh ia ka bor jong ngi ha ka jinghiar pateng, bad, ka jinglong 
klilem nongsynshar ia ka irnlang sahlang kum ki kur bad ba ka 
jaidbynriew ka don ha ka jingma ba la “shukor u ksuid i’u 
Thawlang ka lawbei.” Haba la pule bniah ia kanc la shem ba 
ka dei ka (satire) - ka jingthoh siat jingmut kaba dei ka 
jingpynkhih jingmut khnang ba ngin im bad ba ngin nyrn long 
ka (lethargic race) ne ka jaidbynriew kaba la thait ialade. Ka 
jingsngcwburom naduh ka dohnud ia i Bah J. D. lyngdoh na 
ka bynta ba i wanrah ia kane ka challenge ia ngi baroh. Khublei. 


Dated Shillong 

26 tarik U Ryrnphang 2002.

kiba ong (wat ia la u jong u l^a mh ngim salia). Kumta

baroh tang sha ka Rwing, bad kumta ngi lyngshing shibun

shisnem. Kumta u Rymmang utei kynjreng halor ka jin jong 
u Kulai, u da bat la u Shabuk ha la ka kti bad kynmaw 
halade ba da kumno u la long kumne kumba u long mynta, 
“The Monarch of all he suruei}'. Kumta katba nang iaid ki 
sngi, ka ing jong u Himot bad i Synjat ryngkat i Fadanmon 
i khun khatduh jongki ka la nangdkhaw bad troin ha baroh 
ki Hang. Teng-teng haba u Kynrong lane u Thanah ki wan 
poi Ing-Kmie haba don ki khep ba ki dei ban wan, ki ialen 
ban kyrkieh ban phet noh, namar ki khmat Rangbah jongki 
ki shon ki thar, haba ki kynmaw ia ki sngi ka jinglong 
khynnah jongki, namar baroh kiei-kiei ki la kylla bran-ran- 
ran na kiba ki ju long ha ka por jongki. Ki pang bad pait ka 
dohnud bad jaw ki ummat barich ha la marwei-marwei, 
hynrei ym don lynti lane lad ban leh ei-ei. Khamtam u 
Kynrong u khun rangbah ka Ing uba shong ha Shillong, 
ym ju kw^ah shuh ban phai ka khmat shaphang ka Hang 
kamon ban kiew sha Nongthymmai, namar ha ka jaka, 
kaba u tip bad la pan na la ka Kmie mynnor ban seng la 
ka kam lajong, hangta la iohi ba la ieng ka Ing concrete 
kaba bun-mala jong ki briew. U shu tlor bad pynjynjar ei 
ia la ka mynsiem lada jia ba u kum F>eit shata. Kumjuh ruh 
ka Ing Kmie, keim shym iohi shuh ia ka rong kaba ka ju 
iohi mania ka snem lada ha ka Tnum ne ha ki jingkhang 
bad jingkhang-it, ryngkat ka Kper ka Phrah kam don shuh
